SUM UP ^3EPaquji

It moves data between two devices that are directly connected on the same local network (WiFi, Ethernet, etc.). ^gGBEf9KB

Moving a frame one hop = Sending a chunk of data from one device directly to the next device (like from your laptop to your router, or router to modem). ^aYoAfg97

NIC = The physical chip/port that connects your computer to a network and gives it a unique MAC address. ^SpvFygq4

MAC = A unique, permanent hardware address burned into your network card (NIC) that identifies your device on a local network. ^rV9CpJCW

ARP Reply = The device that owns the requested IP says: "Yes, that's me — here's my MAC address." ^ZvxxKYXU

Unicast means it sends the reply only to the device that asked (not to everyone like the request) ^hLwtM8kJ

A trunk is a single cable that carries traffic for MULTIPLE VLANs at the same time between two switches. ^hFd69MYf

SUM ^xvNn1Tnp

HOW TO STUDY: ^4wTUd90O

1. This course is mostly about reading and understanding the concepts. The topics are not very difficult, but you need to understand them well.

2. Focus on how the concepts are connected to each other rather than studying each topic separately. Understanding the big picture will make the course much easier.

3. Study the material regularly and review the concepts from time to time. Once you understand the ideas and their connections, the course becomes very straightforward.
